    22→def _issue(
    23→    fid: str,
    24→    detector: str = "unused",
    25→    status: str = "open",
    26→    file: str = "src/foo.ts",
    27→    suppressed: bool = False,
    28→) -> dict:
    29→    f: dict = {
    30→        "id": fid,
    31→        "detector": detector,
    32→        "status": status,
    33→        "file": file,
    34→    }
    35→    if suppressed:
    36→        f["suppressed"] = True
    37→    return f
    38→
    39→
    40→def _state_with_issues(*issues: dict) -> dict:
    41→    return {
    42→        "issues": {f["id"]: f for f in issues},
    43→        "scan_count": 5,
    44→    }

    51→class TestAutoResolveOutOfScope:
    52→    def test_out_of_scope_issues_are_resolved(self):
    53→        """Issues outside scan_path are auto-resolved, not skipped."""
    54→        existing = {
    55→            "f1": {
    56→                "id": "f1", "status": "open", "file": "supabase/fn.ts",
    57→                "detector": "unused",
    58→            },
    59→            "f2": {
    60→                "id": "f2", "status": "open", "file": "src/app.ts",
    61→                "detector": "unused",
    62→            },
    63→        }
    64→        resolved, _lang, out_of_scope, detectors = auto_resolve_disappeared(
    65→            existing,
    66→            current_ids=set(),
    67→            suspect_detectors=set(),
    68→            now="2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    69→            lang=None,
    70→            scan_path="src",
    71→        )
    72→        # f1 is out of scope and should be auto-resolved
    73→        assert existing["f1"]["status"] == "auto_resolved"
    74→        assert "Out of current scan scope" in existing["f1"]["note"]
    75→        assert out_of_scope == 1

   162→class TestQueueCountingScanPath:
   163→    def test_queue_count_respects_scan_path_from_state(self):
   164→        """build_work_queue auto-reads scan_path from state and filters issues."""
   165→        from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
   166→            QueueBuildOptions,
   167→            build_work_queue,
   168→        )
   169→
   170→        state: dict = {
   171→            "issues": {
   172→                "f1": {
   173→                    "id": "f1", "detector": "unused", "status": "open",
   174→                    "file": "src/a.ts", "tier": 1, "confidence": "high",
   175→                    "summary": "in scope", "detail": {},
   176→                },
   177→                "f2": {
   178→                    "id": "f2", "detector": "unused", "status": "open",
   179→                    "file": "other/b.ts", "tier": 1, "confidence": "high",
   180→                    "summary": "out of scope", "detail": {},
   181→                },
   182→            },
   183→            "scan_path": "src",
   184→            "scan_count": 5,
   185→        }
   186→        # No explicit scan_path — auto-reads "src" from state
   187→        result = build_work_queue(
   188→            state,
   189→            options=QueueBuildOptions(status="open", count=None),
   190→        )
   191→        ids = {item["id"] for item in result["items"]}
   192→        assert "f1" in ids
   193→        assert "f2" not in ids
   194→        assert result["total"] == 1
   195→

   197→        """Without scan_path in state, all issues are returned."""
   198→        from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
   199→            QueueBuildOptions,

   201→        )
   202→
   203→        state: dict = {

   206→                    "id": "f1", "detector": "unused", "status": "open",

   208→                    "summary": "a", "detail": {},

   210→                "f2": {

   213→                    "summary": "b", "detail": {},
   214→                },
   215→            },
   216→            "scan_count": 5,

   225→        """Explicit scan_path on QueueBuildOptions overrides state value."""
   226→        from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
   227→            QueueBuildOptions,

   236→                    "summary": "a", "detail": {},
   237→                },
   238→                "f2": {

   255→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   256→# Fix 3: compute_subjective_visibility respects scan_path + plan
   257→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   258→
   259→class TestSubjectivePolicyScanPathAndPlan:
   260→    def test_scan_path_excludes_out_of_scope_issues(self):
   261→        """Issues outside scan_path don't count toward objective backlog."""
   262→        state = _state_with_issues(
   263→            _issue("f1", "unused", file="src/app.ts"),
   264→            _issue("f2", "unused", file="supabase/fn.ts"),
   265→            _issue("f3", "unused", file="src/lib.ts"),
   266→        )
   267→        # Without scan_path: all 3 count
   268→        policy_all = compute_subjective_visibility(state)
   269→        assert policy_all.objective_count == 3
   270→
   271→        # With scan_path: only 2 in src/ count
   272→        policy_scoped = compute_subjective_visibility(state, scan_path="src")
   273→        assert policy_scoped.objective_count == 2
   274→        assert policy_scoped.has_objective_backlog is True
   275→
   276→    def test_scan_path_drains_backlog_correctly(self):
   277→        """When all in-scope issues are resolved, backlog is drained even
   278→        if out-of-scope issues remain open."""
   279→        state = _state_with_issues(
   280→            _issue("f1", "unused", file="supabase/fn.ts"),
   281→            _issue("f2", "unused", file="supabase/other.ts"),
   282→        )
   283→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, scan_path="src")
   284→        assert policy.objective_count == 0
   285→        assert policy.has_objective_backlog is False
   286→
   287→    def test_plan_skipped_issues_excluded(self):
   288→        """Issues in plan['skipped'] don't count toward objective backlog."""
   289→        state = _state_with_issues(
   290→            _issue("f1", "unused", file="src/a.ts"),
   291→            _issue("f2", "unused", file="src/b.ts"),
   292→            _issue("f3", "unused", file="src/c.ts"),
   293→        )
   294→        plan = {"skipped": {"f1": {"kind": "temporary"}, "f2": {"kind": "permanent"}}}
   295→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=plan)
   296→        assert policy.objective_count == 1  # only f3
   297→
   298→    def test_scan_path_and_plan_combined(self):
   299→        """Both filters applied together: scope + skipped."""
   300→        state = _state_with_issues(
   301→            _issue("f1", "unused", file="src/a.ts"),     # in scope, not skipped → counts
   302→            _issue("f2", "unused", file="src/b.ts"),     # in scope, skipped → excluded
   303→            _issue("f3", "unused", file="other/c.ts"),   # out of scope → excluded
   304→        )
   305→        plan = {"skipped": {"f2": {"kind": "temporary"}}}
   306→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, scan_path="src", plan=plan)
   307→        assert policy.objective_count == 1
   308→        assert policy.has_objective_backlog is True
   309→
   310→    def test_no_params_backward_compat(self):
   311→        """Without new params, behavior is unchanged (all issues counted)."""
   312→        state = _state_with_issues(
   313→            _issue("f1", "unused", file="anywhere/a.ts"),
   314→        )
   315→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state)
   316→        assert policy.objective_count == 1
   317→

   323→class TestWorkflowRunScanItem:
   324→    def test_run_scan_item_injected_when_queue_empty_and_plan_active(self):
   325→        """When queue is empty and plan_start_scores exist, workflow::run-scan appears."""
   326→        from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
   327→            QueueBuildOptions,
   328→            build_work_queue,
   329→        )
   330→
   331→        state: dict = {"issues": {}, "scan_count": 5}
   332→        plan = {
   333→            "plan_start_scores": {"strict": 75.0},
   334→            "queue_order": [],
   335→            "skipped": {},
   336→        }
   337→        result = build_work_queue(
   338→            state,
   339→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   340→                status="open",
   341→                count=None,
   342→                plan=plan,
   343→            ),
   344→        )
   345→        items = result["items"]
   346→        assert len(items) == 1
   347→        assert items[0]["id"] == "workflow::run-scan"
   348→        assert items[0]["kind"] == "workflow_action"
   349→        assert "scan" in items[0]["primary_command"]

   425→    def test_run_scan_does_not_block_scan_preflight(self):
   426→        """The scan preflight gate uses breakdown.actionable which excludes
   427→        workflow items, so workflow::run-scan doesn't create a circular block."""
   428→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   429→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   430→        )
   431→
   432→        state: dict = {"issues": {}, "scan_count": 5}
   433→        plan = {
   434→            "plan_start_scores": {"strict": 75.0},
   435→            "queue_order": [],
   436→            "skipped": {},
   437→        }
   438→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan=plan)
   439→        assert breakdown.queue_total == 1
   440→        assert breakdown.workflow == 1
   441→        assert breakdown.actionable == 0  # gate passes

   477→# Fix 6: queue_guard passes scan_path
   478→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   479→
   480→class TestQueueGuardScanPath:
   481→    def test_queue_guard_respects_scan_path_from_state(self):
   482→        """_check_queue_order_guard uses build_work_queue which auto-reads
   483→        scan_path from state, so out-of-scope items don't appear in the queue."""
   484→        from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard import _check_queue_order_guard
   485→
   486→        state = {
   487→            "issues": {
   488→                "f1": {"id": "f1", "status": "open", "file": "src/a.ts"},
   489→            },
   490→            "scan_path": "src",
   491→        }
   492→        mock_result = {
   493→            "total": 1,
   494→            "items": [{"id": "f1", "kind": "issue"}],
   495→            "grouped": {},
   496→            "new_ids": set(),
   497→        }
   498→        with (
   499→            patch("desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard.has_living_plan", return_value=True),
   500→            patch("desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard.load_plan", return_value={
   501→                "queue_order": ["f1"],
   502→                "skipped": {},
   503→            }),
   504→            patch(
   505→                "desloppify.app.commands.resolve.queue_guard.build_work_queue",
   506→                return_value=mock_result,
   507→            ) as mock_build,
   508→        ):
   509→            # Should not raise — f1 is at front of queue
   510→            result = _check_queue_order_guard(state, ["f1"], "fixed")
   511→            assert result is False
   512→            # Verify build_work_queue was called (scan_path resolved internally)
   513→            mock_build.assert_called_once()
   514→

1→"""Direct tests for scan plan reconciliation orchestration.
     2→
     3→Tests exercise the real reconciliation logic with realistic plan and state
     4→data structures, mocking only at I/O boundaries (load_plan, save_plan).
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from types import SimpleNamespace
    11→
    12→import pytest
    13→
    14→import desloppify.app.commands.scan.plan_reconcile as reconcile_mod
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    17→    StaleDimensionSyncResult,
    18→    UnscoredDimensionSyncResult,
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23→# Helpers
    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→
    26→def _runtime(*, state=None, config=None) -> SimpleNamespace:
    27→    return SimpleNamespace(
    28→        state=state or {},
    29→        state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"),
    30→        config=config or {},
    31→    )
    32→
    33→
    34→def _make_state(
    35→    *,
    36→    issues: dict | None = None,
    37→    overall_score: float | None = None,
    38→    objective_score: float | None = None,
    39→    strict_score: float | None = None,
    40→    verified_strict_score: float | None = None,
    41→    scan_count: int = 1,
    42→) -> dict:
    43→    """Build a minimal but realistic state dict."""
    44→    state: dict = {
    45→        "issues": issues or {},
    46→        "scan_count": scan_count,
    47→        "dimension_scores": {},
    48→        "subjective_assessments": {},
    49→    }
    50→    if overall_score is not None:
    51→        state["overall_score"] = overall_score
    52→    if objective_score is not None:
    53→        state["objective_score"] = objective_score
    54→    if strict_score is not None:
    55→        state["strict_score"] = strict_score
    56→    if verified_strict_score is not None:
    57→        state["verified_strict_score"] = verified_strict_score
    58→    return state
    59→
    60→
    61→def _make_issue(
    62→    detector: str = "complexity",
    63→    file: str = "src/app.py",
    64→    status: str = "open",
    65→    **extra,
    66→) -> dict:
    67→    return {"detector": detector, "file": file, "status": status, **extra}
    68→
    69→

    71→# Tests: _plan_has_user_content

    74→class TestPlanHasUserContent:

    76→    def test_empty_plan_has_no_user_content(self):

    78→        assert reconcile_mod._plan_has_user_content(plan) is False
    79→

    82→        plan["queue_order"] = ["issue-1"]
    83→        assert reconcile_mod._plan_has_user_content(plan) is True

    41→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    43→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    44→    state = runtime.state
    45→
    46→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    47→        return
    48→
    49→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    50→    if config_warning:
    51→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    52→
    53→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    54→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    55→
    56→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    57→    if output:
    58→        try:
    59→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    60→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    61→            print_agent_plan(
    62→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    63→                next_command=next_command,
    64→            )
    65→        except OSError as e:
    66→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    67→    else:
    68→        print(plan_md)
    69→        print()
    70→        print_agent_plan(
    71→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    72→            next_command=next_command,
    73→        )
    74→
    75→
    76→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    77→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    78→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    82→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    83→    plan = load_plan()
    84→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    85→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    86→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    87→    kind_counts = {
    88→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
    89→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
    90→    }
    91→    temp_count = kind_counts["temporary"]
    92→    perm_count = kind_counts["permanent"]
    93→    fp_count = kind_counts["false_positive"]
    94→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    95→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    96→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    97→
    98→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
    99→
   100→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   101→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   102→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
   103→    if total_skipped:
   104→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   105→    else:
   106→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   107→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   108→    if clusters:
   109→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   110→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   111→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   112→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   113→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   114→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   115→    if described or noted:
   116→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   117→    if active:
   118→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   119→    if superseded:
   120→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
   121→
   122→    # Commit tracking summary
   123→    _cfg = load_config()
   124→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   125→        ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   126→        if ct["total"] > 0:
   127→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   128→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   129→            print(
   130→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   131→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   132→            )
   133→
   134→
   135→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   136→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   137→    plan = load_plan()
   138→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   139→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   140→    reset_plan(plan)
   141→    append_log_entry(
   142→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   143→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   144→    )
   145→    save_plan(plan)
   146→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   147→
   148→
   149→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   150→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   151→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   152→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   153→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   154→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   155→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   156→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   157→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   158→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   159→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   160→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   161→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   162→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   163→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   164→}
   165→
   166→
   167→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   168→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   169→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   170→    if plan_action is None:
   171→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   175→    if handler is None:
   176→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   177→        return
   178→    handler(args)
   179→
   180→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   181→

1→"""Queue mutation helpers for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import add_promoted_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     7→
     8→
     9→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str) -> None:
    10→    """Remove a issue ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    11→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    12→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    13→    if issue_id in order:
    14→        order.remove(issue_id)
    15→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _resolve_position(
    19→    order: list[str],
    20→    position: str,
    21→    target: str | None = None,
    22→    offset: int | None = None,
    23→    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    24→) -> int:
    25→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    26→
    27→    *issue_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    28→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    29→    """
    30→    moving = set(issue_ids or [])
    31→
    32→    if position == "top":
    33→        return 0
    34→    if position == "bottom":
    35→        return len(order)
    36→
    37→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    38→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    39→
    40→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    41→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    42→            order,
    43→            moving,
    44→            position=position,
    45→            offset=offset,
    46→            issue_ids=issue_ids,
    47→        )
    48→
    49→    return len(order)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _resolve_relative_position(
    53→    order: list[str],
    54→    moving: set[str],
    55→    *,
    56→    position: str,
    57→    target: str,
    58→) -> int:
    59→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    60→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    61→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
    62→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
    63→
    64→
    65→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
    66→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
    67→        if item_id == target:
    68→            return i
    69→    return None
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_offset_position(
    73→    order: list[str],
    74→    moving: set[str],
    75→    *,
    76→    position: str,
    77→    offset: int,
    78→    issue_ids: list[str] | None,
    79→) -> int:
    80→    if not issue_ids:
    81→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
    82→    first_id = issue_ids[0]
    83→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    84→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
    85→    if current_idx is None:
    86→        if position == "up":
    87→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    88→        return len(clean_order)
    89→    if position == "up":
    90→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    91→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    92→
    93→
    94→def move_items(
    95→    plan: PlanModel,
    96→    issue_ids: list[str],
    97→    position: str,
    98→    target: str | None = None,
    99→    offset: int | None = None,
   100→) -> int:
   101→    """Move issue IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   102→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   103→
   104→    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered
   105→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS
   106→
   107→    issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]
   108→    if not issue_ids:
   109→        return 0
   110→
   111→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   112→
   113→    # Remove from skipped if present
   114→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   115→    for fid in issue_ids:
   116→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   117→
   118→    # Remove from current position in order
   119→    for fid in issue_ids:
   120→        if fid in order:
   121→            order.remove(fid)
   122→
   123→    # Resolve insertion point
   124→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)
   125→
   126→    # Insert in original order
   127→    for i, fid in enumerate(issue_ids):
   128→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   129→
   130→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   131→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   132→    if position == "top":
   133→        add_promoted_ids(plan, issue_ids)
   134→
   135→    return len(issue_ids)
   136→
   137→
   138→__all__ = ["move_items"]
   139→

1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    10→    QueueBuildOptions,
    11→    WorkQueueResult,
    12→    build_work_queue,
    13→)
    14→
    15→
    16→def _subjective_threshold(state: StateModel, *, default: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE) -> float:
    17→    config = state.get("config", {})
    18→    raw_target = default
    19→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    20→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    21→    try:
    22→        value = float(raw_target)
    23→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    24→        value = default
    25→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    29→class OpenPlanQueuePolicy:
    30→    count: int | None = None
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    include_subjective: bool = True
    33→
    34→
    35→def build_open_plan_queue(
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    policy: OpenPlanQueuePolicy | None = None,
    38→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    39→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    40→    policy = policy or OpenPlanQueuePolicy()
    41→    # When policy.scan_path is explicitly set, override the auto-default.
    42→    # Otherwise let QueueBuildOptions read from state automatically.
    43→    scan_path_kwarg: dict = (
    44→        {"scan_path": policy.scan_path} if policy.scan_path is not None else {}
    45→    )
    46→    return build_work_queue(
    47→        state,
    48→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    49→            count=policy.count,
    50→            status="open",
    51→            include_subjective=policy.include_subjective,
    52→            subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state),
    53→            **scan_path_kwarg,
    54→        ),
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→__all__ = ["OpenPlanQueuePolicy", "build_open_plan_queue"]
    59→

    21→
    22→
    23→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    24→    if len(text) <= width:
    25→        return text
    26→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _print_queue_header(
    30→    *,
    31→    items: list[dict],
    32→    include_skipped: bool,
    33→    plan: dict,
    34→    new_count: int = 0,
    35→) -> None:
    36→    total = len(items)
    37→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    38→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    39→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    42→    subjective = sum(
    43→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    44→    )
    45→
    46→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    47→    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    48→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    49→    plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    50→
    51→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    52→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    53→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    54→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    55→        subjective=subjective,
    56→    )
    57→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    58→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    59→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    60→
    61→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster")
    62→    if focus:
    63→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    64→
    65→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    66→        return
    67→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    68→        return
    69→    print(
    70→        colorize(
    71→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    72→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    73→            "dim",
    74→        )
    75→    )
    76→
    77→
    78→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    79→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    80→        return items[:top]
    81→    return items
    82→
    83→
    84→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    85→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    86→    _new = new_ids or set()
    87→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    88→        pos = str(idx)
    89→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
    90→
    91→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
    92→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
    93→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
    94→            conf_str = "—"
    95→            detector = "planning"
    96→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
    97→            cluster_name = ""
    98→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
    99→            conf_str = "—"
   100→            detector = "workflow"
   101→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   102→            cluster_name = ""
   103→        elif kind == "cluster":
   104→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "high")
   105→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   106→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   107→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   108→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   109→                if m.get("id") in _new
   110→            )
   111→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   112→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   113→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   114→        else:
   115→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   116→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   117→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   118→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   119→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   120→
   121→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   122→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   123→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   124→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   125→    return rows
   126→
   127→
   128→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   129→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   130→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   131→    state = runtime.state
   132→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   133→        return
   134→
   135→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   136→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   137→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   138→
   139→    plan = load_plan()
   140→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   141→
   142→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
   143→    if not cluster_filter:
   144→        active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   145→        if active_cluster:
   146→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   147→
   148→    queue = build_work_queue(
   149→        state,
   150→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   151→            count=None,
   152→            status="open",
   153→            include_subjective=True,
   154→            plan=plan,
   155→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   156→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   157→        ),
   158→    )
   159→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   160→    # Collapse auto-clusters into display meta-items
   161→    if plan and not effective_cluster and not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   162→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   163→
   164→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   165→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   166→    # Merge review-based new issue IDs (since last triage)
   167→    review_new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   168→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   169→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   170→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   171→
   172→    if sort_by == "recent":
   173→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   174→
   175→    _print_queue_header(
   176→        items=items,
   177→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   178→        plan=plan,
   179→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   180→    )
   181→
   182→    if not items:
   183→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    # Determine which items to show
   187→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   188→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   189→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   190→
   191→    print()
   192→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   193→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   194→
   195→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   196→        remaining = len(items) - top
   197→        print(colorize(
   198→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   199→        ))
   200→    print()
   201→
   202→
   203→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   204→

    53→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers

## Summary

I found the bug in the `plan show` command. Here are the key findings:

### File Location
**`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py`**

### The Problem

**Line 84 in `_cmd_plan_show()` function:**
```python
ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
```

This line reads the raw `queue_order` list length directly from the plan, which gives a static count of items in the stored queue_order field. However, this doesn't account for:

1. **Skipped items** - Items marked as skipped should not be counted in the active queue
2. **Dynamic queue filtering** - The actual queue shown to the user is computed dynamically through `build_work_queue()` which applies multiple filters and enrichments

**Line 102 in the same function:**
```python
print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
```

This uses the incorrectly counted value to display the queue size.

### The Correct Approach

Looking at `cmd_plan_queue()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py` (lines 47-49), the proper way to count queue items is:

```python
queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
```

This filters out skipped items from the queue_order count.

### Better Solution

The ideal fix would use `build_work_queue()` (like the queue_render command does) to get the dynamically computed queue with all filters and enrichments applied. This would give the true count of items that will be shown to the user in the actual queue display.

     ```python
     class ScoreDisplayMode(enum.Enum):
         FROZEN = "frozen"
         PHASE_TRANSITION = "phase_transition"
         LIVE = "live"

     def score_display_mode(
         breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
         plan_start_strict: float | None,
     ) -> ScoreDisplayMode:
         if plan_start_strict is None:
             return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
         if breakdown is None:
             return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
         if breakdown.objective_actionable > 0:
             return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
         if breakdown.queue_total > 0:
             return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
         return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE

     def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
         breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
         *,
         frozen_strict: float,
         live_score: float | None = None,
     ) -> None:
         # Simplified — only called from FROZEN path
     ```

     def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
         if _has_initial_reviews(items):
             # Phase 1: only initial reviews + workflow items visible
             return [
                 i for i in items
                 if (i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review"))
                 or i.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
             ]
         if not _has_objective_items(items):
             return items  # endgame: everything visible
         return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]
     ```

Updated `test_unassessed_subjective_visible_with_objective_backlog` in `work_queue_cases.py` from asserting 5 items (4 objective + 1 review) to 1 item (review only). **Tests have NOT been run yet.**

9. Optional Next Step:
   Run the full test suite to verify the lifecycle filter change doesn't break other tests:
   ```
   python -m pytest desloppify/tests/ -q
   ```
   The user's most recent request was: "it should show all subjective reviews and only show them other items after they're all done / they should be in a cluster". The lifecycle gating change addresses "only show them other items after they're all done" but the clustering aspect ("they should be in a cluster") may need additional work if the `auto/initial-review` cluster isn't automatically created for new users. After tests pass, verify the clustering behavior works correctly for the initial-review case.

1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_triage_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
     9→    QueueBreakdown,
    10→    format_queue_headline,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    14→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize, print_table
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    16→    QueueBuildOptions,
    17→    build_work_queue,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    20→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_issue_ids, load_plan
    21→
    22→
    23→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    24→    if len(text) <= width:
    25→        return text
    26→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _print_queue_header(
    30→    *,
    31→    items: list[dict],
    32→    include_skipped: bool,
    33→    plan: dict,
    34→    new_count: int = 0,
    35→) -> None:
    36→    total = len(items)
    37→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    38→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    39→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    42→    subjective = sum(
    43→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    44→    )
    45→
    46→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    47→    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    48→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    49→    plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    50→
    51→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    52→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    53→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    54→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    55→        subjective=subjective,
    56→    )
    57→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    58→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    59→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    60→
    61→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster")
    62→    if focus:
    63→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    64→
    65→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    66→        return
    67→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    68→        return
    69→    print(
    70→        colorize(
    71→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    72→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    73→            "dim",
    74→        )
    75→    )
    76→
    77→
    78→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    79→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    80→        return items[:top]
    81→    return items
    82→
    83→
    84→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    85→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    86→    _new = new_ids or set()
    87→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    88→        pos = str(idx)
    89→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
    90→
    91→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
    92→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
    93→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
    94→            conf_str = "—"
    95→            detector = "planning"
    96→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
    97→            cluster_name = ""
    98→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
    99→            conf_str = "—"
   100→            detector = "workflow"
   101→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   102→            cluster_name = ""
   103→        elif kind == "cluster":
   104→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "high")
   105→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   106→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   107→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   108→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   109→                if m.get("id") in _new
   110→            )
   111→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   112→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   113→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   114→        else:
   115→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   116→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   117→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   118→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   119→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   120→
   121→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   122→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   123→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   124→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   125→    return rows
   126→
   127→
   128→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   129→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   130→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   131→    state = runtime.state
   132→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   133→        return
   134→
   135→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   136→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   137→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   138→
   139→    plan = load_plan()
   140→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   141→
   142→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
   143→    if not cluster_filter:
   144→        active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   145→        if active_cluster:
   146→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   147→
   148→    queue = build_work_queue(
   149→        state,
   150→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   151→            count=None,
   152→            status="open",
   153→            include_subjective=True,
   154→            plan=plan,
   155→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   156→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   157→        ),
   158→    )
   159→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   160→    # Collapse auto-clusters into display meta-items
   161→    if plan and not effective_cluster and not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   162→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   163→
   164→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   165→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   166→    # Merge review-based new issue IDs (since last triage)
   167→    review_new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   168→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   169→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   170→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   171→
   172→    if sort_by == "recent":
   173→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   174→
   175→    _print_queue_header(
   176→        items=items,
   177→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   178→        plan=plan,
   179→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   180→    )
   181→
   182→    if not items:
   183→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    # Determine which items to show
   187→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   188→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   189→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   190→
   191→    print()
   192→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   193→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   194→
   195→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   196→        remaining = len(items) - top
   197→        print(colorize(
   198→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   199→        ))
   200→    print()
   201→
   202→
   203→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   204→

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT = 25
    12→
    13→
    14→def _serialize_cluster_member(member: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    15→    """Serialize a cluster member without nested plan metadata."""
    16→    return {
    17→        "id": member.get("id"),
    18→        "kind": member.get("kind", "issue"),
    19→        "confidence": member.get("confidence"),
    20→        "detector": member.get("detector"),
    21→        "file": member.get("file"),
    22→        "summary": member.get("summary"),
    23→        "status": member.get("status"),
    24→        "primary_command": member.get("primary_command"),
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    29→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    30→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    31→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    32→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    33→        serialized_members = [
    34→            _serialize_cluster_member(member)
    35→            for member in members_raw[:_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT]
    36→        ]
    37→        member_count = int(item.get("member_count", len(members_raw)))
    38→        serialized_cluster: dict[str, Any] = {
    39→            "id": item.get("id"),
    40→            "kind": "cluster",
    41→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    42→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    43→            "member_count": member_count,
    44→            "members": serialized_members,
    45→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    46→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    47→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    48→        }
    49→        if member_count > len(serialized_members):
    50→            serialized_cluster["members_truncated"] = True
    51→            serialized_cluster["members_sample_limit"] = _CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT
    52→        serialized_cluster["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    53→        return serialized_cluster
    54→
    55→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    56→        "id": item.get("id"),
    57→        "kind": item.get("kind", "issue"),
    58→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    59→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    60→        "file": item.get("file"),
    61→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    62→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    63→        "status": item.get("status"),
    64→    }
    65→    serialized["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    66→
    67→    # Workflow dependency state
    68→    if item.get("blocked_by"):
    69→        serialized["blocked_by"] = item["blocked_by"]
    70→    if item.get("is_blocked"):
    71→        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    72→
    73→    explain = item.get("explain")
    74→    if explain is not None:
    75→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    76→
    77→    # Plan metadata
    78→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    79→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    80→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    81→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    82→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    83→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    84→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    85→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    86→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    87→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    88→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    89→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    90→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    91→
    92→    return serialized
    93→
    94→
    95→def build_query_payload(
    96→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    97→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    98→    *,
    99→    command: str,
   100→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
   101→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   102→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   103→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
   104→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
   105→    queue_section: dict[str, Any] = {
   106→        "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
   107→    }
   108→
   109→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   110→        "command": command,
   111→        "items": serialized,
   112→        "queue": queue_section,
   113→        "narrative": narrative,
   114→    }
   115→
   116→    if plan and (
   117→        plan.get("queue_order")
   118→        or plan.get("skipped")
   119→        or plan.get("clusters")
   120→    ):
   121→        clusters_summary = []
   122→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   123→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   124→            clusters_summary.append({
   125→                "name": name,
   126→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   127→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   128→            })
   129→        payload["plan"] = {
   130→            "active": True,
   131→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   132→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   133→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   134→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   135→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   136→        }
   137→
   138→    return payload
   139→
   140→
   141→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   142→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   143→    lines = [
   144→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   145→        "",
   146→        "| Kind | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   147→        "|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   148→    ]
   149→    for item in items:
   150→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   151→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   152→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   153→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   154→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   155→    lines.append("")
   156→    return "\n".join(lines)
   157→
   158→
   159→def write_output_file(
   160→    output_file: str,
   161→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   162→    item_count: int,
   163→    *,
   164→    safe_write_text_fn,
   165→    colorize_fn,
   166→) -> bool:
   167→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   168→    try:
   169→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   170→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   171→    except OSError as exc:
   172→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   173→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   174→        return False
   175→    return True
   176→
   177→
   178→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   179→    output_format: str,
   180→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   181→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   182→) -> bool:
   183→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   184→    renderers = {
   185→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   186→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   187→    }
   188→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   189→    if renderer is None:
   190→        return False
   191→    renderer()
   192→    return True
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = [
   196→    "build_query_payload",
   197→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   198→    "render_markdown",
   199→    "serialize_item",
   200→    "write_output_file",
   201→]
   202→
```

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import enum
     6→import importlib
     7→import logging
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    10→
    11→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    13→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    14→from desloppify.engine import plan as plan_mod
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue import core as work_queue_core_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import is_subjective_queue_item
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    18→
    19→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    20→
    21→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    22→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    23→
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    30→class QueueBreakdown:
    31→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    32→
    33→    queue_total: int = 0
    34→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    35→    skipped: int = 0
    36→    subjective: int = 0
    37→    workflow: int = 0
    38→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    39→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    40→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    41→
    42→    @property
    43→    def objective_actionable(self) -> int:
    44→        """Objective items only — excludes subjective and workflow.
    45→
    46→        This is the count used by :func:`score_display_mode` to decide
    47→        frozen vs live display.  Do not use it directly for gating —
    48→        use ``score_display_mode()`` instead.
    49→        """
    50→        return max(0, self.queue_total - self.subjective - self.workflow)
    51→
    52→
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→# ScoreDisplayMode — single decision point for frozen vs live score display
    55→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    56→
    57→class ScoreDisplayMode(enum.Enum):
    58→    """How to display the score given the current plan cycle and queue state.
    59→
    60→    - ``FROZEN``: objective work remains → show frozen plan-start score.
    61→    - ``PHASE_TRANSITION``: objective drained, subjective/workflow remain
    62→      → show live scores + transition banner.
    63→    - ``LIVE``: no active plan cycle or queue fully clear → show live scores.
    64→    """
    65→    FROZEN = "frozen"
    66→    PHASE_TRANSITION = "phase_transition"
    67→    LIVE = "live"
    68→
    69→
    70→def score_display_mode(
    71→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
    72→    plan_start_strict: float | None,
    73→) -> ScoreDisplayMode:
    74→    """Determine the score display mode from breakdown + plan-start score.
    75→
    76→    This is the **single source of truth** for the frozen-vs-live decision.
    77→    Every call site (status, scan reveal, next nudges, plan reconcile, plan
    78→    nudge) must use this instead of ad-hoc count checks.
    79→    """
    80→    if plan_start_strict is None:
    81→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
    82→    if breakdown is None:
    83→        return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
    84→    if breakdown.objective_actionable > 0:
    85→        return ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
    86→    if breakdown.queue_total > 0:
    87→        return ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION
    88→    return ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE
    89→
    90→
    91→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    92→    state: dict,
    93→    plan: dict | None = None,
    94→    *,
    95→    context: QueueContext | None = None,
    96→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    97→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call.
    98→
    99→    When *context* is provided, its ``plan`` and ``policy`` are forwarded to
   100→    ``build_work_queue`` so the counts agree with the caller's resolution.
   101→    """
   102→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan
   103→
   104→    result = work_queue_core_mod.build_work_queue(
   105→        state,
   106→        options=work_queue_core_mod.QueueBuildOptions(
   107→            status="open",
   108→            count=None,
   109→            plan=effective_plan if context is None else None,
   110→            include_skipped=False,
   111→            context=context,
   112→        ),
   113→    )
   114→
   115→    # Collapse clusters for display-level counting
   116→    items = result.get("items", [])
   117→    if effective_plan and not effective_plan.get("active_cluster"):
   118→        items = collapse_clusters(items, effective_plan)
   119→
   120→    queue_total = len(items)
   121→
   122→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
   123→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
   124→    subjective = sum(
   125→        1 for item in items
   126→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
   127→    )
   128→    workflow = sum(
   129→        1 for item in items
   130→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    # Plan-derived counts
   134→    plan_ordered = 0
   135→    skipped = 0
   136→    if effective_plan:
   137→        skipped = len(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}))
   138→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
   139→        queue_order = effective_plan.get("queue_order", [])
   140→        skipped_ids = set(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   141→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
   142→
   143→    # Focus cluster info
   144→    focus_cluster = None
   145→    focus_cluster_count = 0
   146→    focus_cluster_total = 0
   147→    if effective_plan:
   148→        active = effective_plan.get("active_cluster")
   149→        if active:
   150→            focus_cluster = active
   151→            cluster_data = effective_plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
   152→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   153→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
   154→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   155→            open_issues = {
   156→                fid
   157→                for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   158→                if f.get("status") == "open"
   159→            }
   160→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_issues)
   161→
   162→    return QueueBreakdown(
   163→        queue_total=queue_total,
   164→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
   165→        skipped=skipped,
   166→        subjective=subjective,
   167→        workflow=workflow,
   168→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
   169→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
   170→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→
   174→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   175→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   176→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→
   178→def format_plan_delta(live: float, frozen: float) -> str:
   179→    """Format plan-start vs live delta, or '' if below threshold."""
   180→    if abs(live - frozen) < 0.05:
   181→        return ""
   182→    delta = round(live - frozen, 1)
   183→    return f"{'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f}"
   184→
   185→
   186→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   187→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   188→
   189→    Examples::
   190→
   191→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   192→        Queue: 1934 items
   193→    """
   194→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   195→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   196→
   197→    # Parenthesized segments
   198→    segments: list[str] = []
   199→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   200→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   201→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   202→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   203→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   204→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   205→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   206→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   207→    if segments:
   208→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   209→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   210→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   211→    return label
   212→
   213→
   214→def format_queue_block(
   215→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   216→    *,
   217→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   218→    live_score: float | None = None,
   219→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   220→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   221→
   222→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   223→    """
   224→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   225→
   226→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   227→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   228→        focus_line = (
   229→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   230→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   231→            f" items remaining"
   232→        )
   233→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   234→
   235→    # Score line: show both frozen plan-start and live score when available
   236→    if frozen_score is not None:
   237→        delta_str = format_plan_delta(live_score, frozen_score) if live_score is not None else ""
   238→        if delta_str:
   239→            lines.append((
   240→                f"  Score: strict {live_score:.1f}/100 (plan start: {frozen_score:.1f}, {delta_str})",
   241→                "cyan",
   242→            ))
   243→        else:
   244→            lines.append((
   245→                f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   246→                "cyan",
   247→            ))
   248→
   249→    # Queue headline — always the same
   250→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   251→
   252→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   253→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   254→        lines.append((
   255→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   256→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   257→            "dim",
   258→        ))
   259→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   260→        lines.append((
   261→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   262→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   263→            "dim",
   264→        ))
   265→    else:
   266→        lines.append((
   267→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   268→            "dim",
   269→        ))
   270→
   271→    return lines
   272→
   273→
   274→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   275→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   276→    if not plan:
   277→        return None
   278→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   279→
   280→
   281→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   282→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   283→    *,
   284→    frozen_strict: float,
   285→    live_score: float | None = None,
   286→) -> None:
   287→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   288→
   289→    Only call from a ``ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN`` path — assumes objective
   290→    work remains in the queue.
   291→    """
   292→    block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=frozen_strict, live_score=live_score)
   293→    print()
   294→    for text, style in block:
   295→        print(colorize(text, style))
   296→    print(colorize(
   297→        "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   298→        "dim",
   299→    ))
   300→
   301→
   302→def print_objective_drained_banner(
   303→    frozen_strict: float,
   304→    remaining: int,
   305→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   306→) -> None:
   307→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   308→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   309→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   310→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   311→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   312→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   313→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   314→    print(colorize(
   315→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   316→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   317→        "cyan",
   318→    ))
   319→    print(colorize(
   320→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   321→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   322→        "dim",
   323→    ))
   324→
   325→
   326→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   327→    state: dict,
   328→    prev,
   329→    plan: dict | None,
   330→) -> None:
   331→    """Context-aware score display using :func:`score_display_mode`."""
   332→    frozen_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
   333→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   334→
   335→    if frozen_strict is not None:
   336→        try:
   337→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   338→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   339→            _logger.debug("queue breakdown computation skipped", exc_info=True)
   340→
   341→    mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict)
   342→
   343→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN:
   344→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   345→            breakdown,
   346→            frozen_strict=frozen_strict,
   347→            live_score=state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict,
   348→        )
   349→        return
   350→
   351→    # LIVE or PHASE_TRANSITION: show current scores
   352→    score_update_mod = importlib.import_module(
   353→        "desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update"
   354→    )
   355→    score_update_mod.print_score_update(state, prev)
   356→
   357→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.PHASE_TRANSITION:
   358→        print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, breakdown.queue_total, breakdown)
   359→
   360→
   361→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   362→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   363→
   364→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   365→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   366→    """
   367→    try:
   368→        plan = plan_mod.load_plan()
   369→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   370→        plan = None
   371→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   372→
   373→
   374→__all__ = [
   375→    "QueueBreakdown",
   376→    "ScoreDisplayMode",
   377→    "format_plan_delta",
   378→    "format_queue_block",
   379→    "format_queue_headline",
   380→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   381→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   382→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   383→    "print_objective_drained_banner",
   384→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   385→    "score_display_mode",
   386→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   387→]
