    41→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    42→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    43→
    44→# Sentinel: "read scan_path from state" (the safe default).
    45→# Callers that want to override can pass an explicit str or None.
    46→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE = object()
    47→
    48→
    49→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    50→class QueueBuildOptions:
    51→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    52→
    53→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    54→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    55→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    56→    """
    57→
    58→    # Output control
    59→    count: int | None = 1
    60→    explain: bool = False
    61→
    62→    # Scope filtering
    63→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    64→    scope: str | None = None
    65→    status: str = "open"
    66→    chronic: bool = False
    67→
    68→    # Subjective gating
    69→    include_subjective: bool = True
    70→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    71→
    72→    # Plan integration
    73→    plan: dict | None = None
    74→    include_skipped: bool = False
    75→    cluster: str | None = None
    76→
    77→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan)
    78→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    79→
    80→
    81→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    82→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    83→
    84→    items: list[WorkQueueItem]
    85→    total: int
    86→    grouped: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]]
    87→    new_ids: set[str]
    88→
    89→
    90→def build_work_queue(
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    94→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    95→    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.
    96→
    97→    Pipeline:
    98→    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    99→    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom, apply floor
   100→    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
   101→    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
   102→    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
   103→    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   104→    """
   105→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   106→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   107→
   108→    # 1. Gather
   109→    items = build_issue_items(
   110→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   111→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   112→    )
   113→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
   114→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   115→
   116→    # 2. Score & filter
   117→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   118→    items = [i for i in items if _passes_impact_floor(i)]
   119→
   120→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
   121→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   122→
   123→    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
   124→    items = _apply_lifecycle_filter(items)
   125→
   126→    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
   127→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   128→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   129→
   130→    # 6. Finalize
   131→    if not items:
   132→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   133→    total = len(items)
   134→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   135→        items = items[:opts.count]
   136→    if opts.explain:
   137→        for item in items:
   138→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   139→
   140→    return {
   141→        "items": items,
   142→        "total": total,
   143→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   144→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   145→    }
   146→
   147→
   148→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   150→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   151→
   152→
   153→def _resolve_inputs(
   154→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   155→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   156→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   157→    ctx = opts.context
   158→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   159→
   160→    scan_path: str | None = (
   161→        state.get("scan_path")
   162→        if opts.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   163→        else opts.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   164→    )
   165→
   166→    status = opts.status
   167→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   168→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   169→
   170→    try:
   171→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   172→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   173→        threshold = 100.0
   174→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   175→
   176→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   177→
   178→
   179→def _gather_subjective_items(
   180→    state: StateModel,
   181→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   182→    threshold: float,
   183→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   184→    """Build subjective dimension candidates.
   185→
   186→    Lifecycle filtering (endgame gating) happens in _apply_lifecycle_filter,
   187→    not here. This function only handles configuration and scope.
   188→    """
   189→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   190→        return []
   191→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   192→        return []
   193→    if opts.chronic:
   194→        return []
   195→
   196→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   197→        state, state.get("issues", {}), threshold=threshold,
   198→    )
   199→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
   200→
   201→
   202→def _gather_workflow_items(
   203→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   204→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   205→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   206→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
   207→        return []
   208→
   209→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
   210→    for builder in (
   211→        build_score_checkpoint_item,
   212→        build_import_scores_item,
   213→        build_communicate_score_item,
   214→    ):
   215→        item = builder(plan, state)
   216→        if item is not None:
   217→            items.append(item)
   218→    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
   219→    if plan_item is not None:
   220→        items.append(plan_item)
   221→    return items
   222→
   223→
   224→_MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   225→
   226→
   227→def _passes_impact_floor(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   228→    """Return True if item should survive the impact floor filter."""
   229→    if item.get("kind") != "issue":
   230→        return True
   231→    if item.get("is_review") or item.get("is_subjective"):
   232→        return True
   233→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact")
   234→    return not impact or impact >= _MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   235→
   236→
   237→def _plan_presort(
   238→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], state: StateModel, plan: dict | None,
   239→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   240→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting.
   241→
   242→    Returns ``(new_ids, skipped)`` — skipped items are removed from
   243→    ``items`` in place and returned separately for post-sort re-append.
   244→    """
   245→    if not plan:
   246→        return set(), []
   247→
   248→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   249→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   250→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
   251→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
   252→    items[:] = remaining
   253→    return new_ids, skipped
   254→
   255→
   256→def _plan_postsort(
   257→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   258→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
   259→    plan: dict | None,
   260→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   261→) -> None:
   262→    """Re-append skipped items, stamp positions, filter to cluster focus."""
   263→    if not plan:
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    if opts.include_skipped:
   267→        items.extend(skipped)
   268→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   269→    focused = filter_cluster_focus(items, plan, opts.cluster)
   270→    items[:] = focused
   271→
   272→
   273→def _has_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   274→    """True if any objective mechanical work items remain in the queue."""
   275→    return any(
   276→        i.get("kind") == "issue"
   277→        and i.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   278→        for i in items
   279→    )
   280→
   281→
   282→def _has_initial_reviews(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   283→    """True if any unassessed subjective dimensions need initial review."""
   284→    return any(
   285→        i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   286→        and i.get("initial_review")
   287→        for i in items
   288→    )
   289→
   290→
   291→def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   292→    """True if this item should only appear when the objective queue is drained."""
   293→    return (
   294→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   295→        and not item.get("initial_review")
   296→    )
   297→
   298→
   299→def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   300→    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules.
   301→
   302→    The queue enforces a phase order: scan → initial review → objective work.
   303→
   304→    1. **Initial reviews pending** → only show initial-review subjective items.
   305→       The user must complete first-time reviews before working objective items.
   306→    2. **Objective work remains** → show objective items, hide endgame-only
   307→       subjective reassessments (stale re-reviews).
   308→    3. **Objective drained** → everything visible (endgame).
   309→    """
   310→    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
   311→        # Phase 1: only initial reviews + workflow items visible
   312→        return [
   313→            i for i in items
   314→            if (i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review"))
   315→            or i.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
   316→        ]
   317→    if not _has_objective_items(items):
   318→        return items  # endgame: everything visible
   319→    return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]
   320→
   321→
   322→def _empty_queue_fallback(plan: dict | None) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   323→    """Return a 'run scan' nudge when an active plan cycle has cleared."""
   324→    if not plan:
   325→        return []
   326→    plan_scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   327→    if plan_scores.get("strict") is None:
   328→        return []
   329→    return [{
   330→        "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   331→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   332→        "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   333→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   334→        "file": "",
   335→        "detector": "workflow",
   336→        "confidence": "high",

## Ordering Bug Analysis: Three Code Paths That Inject Triage Before Subjective Review

I've read all the critical files. Here's what I found:

### 1. **Post-Scan Reconcile Ordering** (`plan_reconcile.py` lines 292-343)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py`

The reconcile function executes syncs in this order:

```python
def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
    # ... loading ...
    
    # Line 305-306: Sync unscored dimensions (initial reviews)
    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
        dirty = True
    
    # Lines 312-318: Sync stale dimensions (refreshed reviews)
    if _sync_stale_and_log(
        plan,
        runtime.state,
        policy=policy,
        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
    ):
        dirty = True
    
    # ... auto_cluster sync ...
    
    # Line 329: **PROBLEM PATH 1** — Triage sync runs BEFORE score/create-plan
    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, runtime.state):
        dirty = True
    
    # Line 331: Score communication sync
    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
        dirty = True
    
    # Line 333: **Create plan sync runs LAST**
    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, runtime.state, policy=policy):
        dirty = True
```

**Issue:** Triage sync (line 329) executes before the subjective review completion syncs. When `sync_triage_needed()` detects new review issues, it **injects triage stages at the front of the queue** (via `_inject_pending_triage_stages`) before score communication and create-plan workflows have been evaluated.

---

### 2. **Triage Stage Insertion at Front** (`stale_dimensions.py` lines 207-224)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`

The function `_inject_pending_triage_stages` **unconditionally inserts triage stages at the front**:

```python
def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
    order: list[str],
    plan: PlanModel,
    confirmed: set[str],
) -> bool:
    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.

    Returns True if any stages were injected.
    """
    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)  # Line 216
    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
    existing = set(order)
    injected_count = 0
    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
            order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)  # Line 222
            injected_count += 1
    return injected_count > 0
```

And the function `sync_triage_needed` calls this when review issues change (lines 341-399):

```python
def sync_triage_needed(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
) -> TriageSyncResult:
    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
    
    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
    ...
    """
    # Lines 386-393
    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)

        if new_since_triage:
            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, plan, confirmed)
```

**Issue:** This uses `promoted_insertion_index()` to find where to insert, which places triage **after any "promoted" items** but **before any unscored subjective dimensions** that were already prepended. This means if unscored dimensions were just prepended by `sync_unscored_dimensions`, triage stages get inserted between them and the rest of the queue.

### 3. **Queue Ranking Priority** (`ranking.py` lines 43, 197-201, 189-205)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`

Natural ranking tiers (lines 43-47):

```python
# Natural ranking groups (_natural_sort_key)
_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3  # Unassessed subjective dimensions
_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -2    # Epic triage workflow stages
_RANK_WORKFLOW = -1         # Score checkpoints, create-plan
_RANK_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered issues
_RANK_ISSUE = 1           # Individual issues + assessed subjective
```

The `_natural_sort_key` function (lines 189-238) applies these ranks:

```python
def _natural_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple:
    """Compute natural (non-plan-aware) ranking for queue items."""
    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")

    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority
    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
        return (_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))

    # **PROBLEM PATH 3** — Triage stage items: get -2 ranking (higher than workflow!)
    if kind == "workflow_stage":
        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
        stage_index = _workflow_stage_index(item)
        return (_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))

    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan) get -1 ranking
    if kind == "workflow_action":
        return (_RANK_WORKFLOW, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
```

**Issue:** Triage stages get `_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -2`, while workflow actions (score checkpoint, communicate-score, create-plan) get `_RANK_WORKFLOW = -1`. Lower numbers sort first, so triage stages **sort higher priority than all workflow actions**, even though the intended lifecycle is: initial review → subjective review → score → create-plan → **triage**.

### 5. **Lifecycle Filter** (`core.py` lines 299-319)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

The lifecycle filter governs what's visible at each phase (lines 299-319):

```python
def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules.

    The queue enforces a phase order: scan → initial review → objective work.

    1. **Initial reviews pending** → only show initial-review subjective items.
       The user must complete first-time reviews before working objective items.
    2. **Objective work remains** → show objective items, hide endgame-only
       subjective reassessments (stale re-reviews).
    3. **Objective drained** → everything visible (endgame).
    """
    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
        # **KEY ISSUE** — Phase 1: only initial reviews + workflow items visible
        return [
            i for i in items
            if (i.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and i.get("initial_review"))
            or i.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")  # <-- triage allowed!
        ]
    if not _has_objective_items(items):
        return items  # endgame: everything visible
    return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]
```

**Issue:** The lifecycle filter at line 315 **allows both `workflow_stage` and `workflow_action` items to pass through during Phase 1** (initial reviews pending). This means triage stages are **not gated by the lifecycle**—they're treated as workflow actions, which are allowed during any phase.

### 6. **Build Work Queue Pipeline** (`core.py` lines 90-145)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

The pipeline that assembles the queue (lines 90-145):

```python
def build_work_queue(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
) -> WorkQueueResult:
    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.

    Pipeline:
    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom, apply floor
    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
    """
    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)

    # 1. Gather
    items = build_issue_items(...)
    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)  # <-- triage gathered here

    # 2. Score & filter
    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
    items = [i for i in items if _passes_impact_floor(i)]

    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)

    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
    items = _apply_lifecycle_filter(items)  # <-- triage NOT filtered

    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)  # <-- triage gets -2 rank, sorts highest
    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)

    # 6. Finalize
```

The `_gather_workflow_items` function (lines 202-221) calls `build_triage_stage_items` first:

```python
def _gather_workflow_items(
    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
        return []

    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))  # Line 209
    for builder in (
        build_score_checkpoint_item,
        build_import_scores_item,
        build_communicate_score_item,
    ):
        item = builder(plan, state)
        if item is not None:
            items.append(item)
    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
    if plan_item is not None:
        items.append(plan_item)
    return items
```

---

## Summary: The Three Ordering Bug Vectors

1. **Reconcile order (plan_reconcile.py)**: `sync_triage_needed()` is called at line 329, **before** `sync_communicate_score_needed()` (line 331) and `sync_create_plan_needed()` (line 333). This means triage stages get injected into the queue before workflow actions have been queued.

2. **Insertion position (stale_dimensions.py)**: The function `_inject_pending_triage_stages()` inserts at `promoted_insertion_index()` (line 216), placing triage stages after any promoted items but **at the front of the normal queue**, potentially before unscored subjective dimensions if they're being prepended in the same pass.

3. **Ranking priority (ranking.py)**: Triage stages get `_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -2`, while all workflow actions (score checkpoint, communicate-score, create-plan) get `_RANK_WORKFLOW = -1`. Since lower numbers sort first, triage **always sorts before workflow actions** in the final queue, even though the intended lifecycle is review → score → create-plan → triage.

The lifecycle filter (`core.py` lines 299-319) doesn't gate triage stages; they pass through during all phases because they're tagged as `workflow_stage` and the filter allows both `workflow_stage` and `workflow_action` during initial reviews.

    46→_RANK_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered issues
    47→_RANK_ISSUE = 1           # Individual issues + assessed subjective
    48→
    49→_TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER = {
    50→    "observe": 0,
    51→    "reflect": 1,
    52→    "organize": 2,
    53→    "commit": 3,
    54→}
    55→
    56→
    57→def _workflow_stage_index(item: WorkQueueItem) -> int:
    58→    raw_index = item.get("stage_index")
    59→    if raw_index is not None:
    60→        try:
    61→            return int(raw_index)
    62→        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
    63→            logger.warning("Invalid workflow stage index %r: %s", raw_index, exc)
    64→    return _TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER.get(workflow_stage_name(item).lower(), 0)
    65→
    66→
    67→def enrich_with_impact(
    68→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    71→
    72→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    73→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    74→    """
    75→    if not dimension_scores:
    76→        for item in items:
    77→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    81→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    82→
    83→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    84→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    85→    for entry in entries:
    86→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    87→        if not name:
    88→            continue
    89→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    90→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    91→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    92→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    93→
    94→    for item in items:
    95→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    96→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    97→
    98→
    99→def _compute_item_impact(
   100→    item: WorkQueueItem,
   101→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
   102→    get_dimension_for_detector,
   103→) -> float:
   104→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
   105→    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   106→
   107→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective issues):
   108→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
   109→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   110→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
   111→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
   112→        if entry:
   113→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   114→        return 0.0
   115→
   116→    # Mechanical issues: use detector -> dimension mapping
   117→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   118→    if detector:
   119→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   120→        if dimension:
   121→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
   122→            if entry:
   123→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   124→
   125→    return 0.0
   126→
   127→
   128→def build_issue_items(
   129→    state: StateModel,
   130→    *,
   131→    scan_path: str | None,
   132→    status_filter: str,
   133→    scope: str | None,
   134→    chronic: bool,
   135→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   136→    scoped = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   137→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   138→    out: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   139→
   140→    for issue_id, issue in scoped.items():
   141→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
   142→            continue
   143→        if not status_matches(issue.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   144→            continue
   145→        if chronic and not (
   146→            issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   147→        ):
   148→            continue
   149→
   150→        # Evidence-only: skip issues below standalone confidence threshold
   151→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
   152→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   153→        if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   154→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   155→            issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   156→            if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
   157→                continue
   158→
   159→        item = cast(WorkQueueItem, dict(issue))
   160→        item["id"] = issue_id
   161→        item["kind"] = "issue"
   162→        item["is_review"] = is_review_issue(item)
   163→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_issue(item)
   164→        item["review_weight"] = (
   165→            review_issue_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   166→        )
   167→        subjective_score = None
   168→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   169→            detail = detail_dict(issue)
   170→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   171→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   172→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   173→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   174→            )
   175→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   176→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   177→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_issue(
   178→            item,
   179→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   180→        )
   181→
   182→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   183→            continue
   184→        out.append(item)
   185→
   186→    return out
   187→
   188→
   189→def _natural_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple:
   190→    """Compute natural (non-plan-aware) ranking for queue items."""
   191→    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   192→
   193→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority
   194→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   195→        return (_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   196→
   197→    # Triage stage items: stage order, blocked after unblocked
   198→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   199→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   200→        stage_index = _workflow_stage_index(item)
   201→        return (_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   202→
   203→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan)
   204→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   205→        return (_RANK_WORKFLOW, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   206→
   207→    if kind == "cluster":
   208→        # Clusters sort before individual issues, ordered by action type
   209→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   210→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   211→        )
   212→        return (
   213→            _RANK_CLUSTER,
   214→            action_pri,
   215→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   216→            item.get("id", ""),
   217→        )
   218→
   219→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)
   220→
   221→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   222→        return (
   223→            _RANK_ISSUE,
   224→            -impact,
   225→            subjective_score_value(item),
   226→            item.get("id", ""),
   227→        )
   228→
   229→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   230→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   231→    return (
   232→        _RANK_ISSUE,
   233→        -impact,
   234→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   235→        -review_weight,
   236→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   237→        item.get("id", ""),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→def item_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple:
   242→    """Unified sort key: plan position first, then natural ranking.
   243→
   244→    When ``_plan_position`` is stamped (by :func:`stamp_plan_sort_keys`),
   245→    planned items sort first in plan order, then existing items by natural
   246→    ranking, then newly-discovered items by natural ranking.
   247→
   248→    When no plan fields are stamped, falls back to pure natural ranking.
   249→    """
   250→    plan_pos = item.get("_plan_position")
   251→
   252→    if plan_pos is not None:
   253→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   254→        # Triage stages: even when explicitly planned, maintain the
   255→        # blocked-before-unblocked invariant for correctness.
   256→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   257→            blocked = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   258→            stage_idx = _workflow_stage_index(item)
   259→            return (_TIER_PLANNED, blocked, stage_idx, item.get("id", ""))
   260→        return (_TIER_PLANNED, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))
   261→
   262→    is_new = item.get("_is_new", False)
   263→    group = _TIER_NEW if is_new else _TIER_EXISTING
   264→    return (group, *_natural_sort_key(item))
   265→
   266→
   267→__all__ = [
   268→    "build_issue_items",
   269→    "enrich_with_impact",
   270→    "item_explain",
   271→    "item_sort_key",
   272→    "subjective_score_value",
   273→    "group_queue_items",
   274→]
   275→

1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import (
     9→    get_issue_description,
    10→    get_issue_note,
    11→    get_issue_override,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    14→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    18→    """Return issue IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    19→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    20→    if not scan_history:
    21→        return set()
    22→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    23→    if not threshold:
    24→        return set()
    25→    return {
    26→        issue_id
    27→        for issue_id, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    28→        if issue.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    29→    }
    30→
    31→
    32→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
    33→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    34→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    35→
    36→    for item in items:
    37→        issue_id = item["id"]
    38→        description = get_issue_description(plan, issue_id)
    39→        note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    40→        override = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id)
    41→        if description:
    42→            item["plan_description"] = description
    43→        if note:
    44→            item["plan_note"] = note
    45→        if override.get("cluster"):
    46→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    47→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    48→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    49→                "name": cluster_name,
    50→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    51→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", [])),
    52→                "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
    53→            }
    54→
    55→
    56→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    plan: dict,
    59→    new_ids: set[str],
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    62→
    63→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    64→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    65→    """
    66→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    67→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    68→
    69→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    70→    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    71→        if issue_id not in skipped_ids:
    72→            position_map[issue_id] = idx
    73→
    74→    for item in items:
    75→        item_id = item["id"]
    76→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    77→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    78→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    79→
    80→
    81→def separate_skipped(
    82→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    83→    plan: dict,
    84→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    85→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    88→    """
    89→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    90→    if not skipped_ids:
    91→        return items, []
    92→    non_skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    93→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    94→    for item in items:
    95→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    96→            skipped.append(item)
    97→        else:
    98→            non_skipped.append(item)
    99→    return non_skipped, skipped
   100→
   101→
   102→def filter_cluster_focus(
   103→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   104→    plan: dict,
   105→    cluster: str | None,
   106→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
   109→    if not effective_cluster:
   110→        return items
   111→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   112→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   113→    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   115→        return items
   116→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   117→
   118→
   119→def stamp_positions(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
   120→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   121→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   122→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   123→
   124→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   125→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   126→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   127→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   128→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   129→            if skip_entry:
   130→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   131→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   132→                if skip_reason:
   133→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   134→
   135→
   136→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   137→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   138→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   139→    if meta:
   140→        return meta.action_type
   141→    return "manual_fix"
   142→
   143→
   144→def _build_cluster_meta(
   145→    cluster_name: str, members: list[WorkQueueItem], cluster_data: dict[str, Any]
   146→) -> WorkQueueItem:
   147→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   148→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   149→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   150→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   151→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   152→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   153→        action_type = "reorganize"
   154→    else:
   155→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   156→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   157→            action_type = "refactor"
   158→
   159→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   160→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   161→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   162→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   163→    else:
   164→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} issues"
   165→
   166→    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   167→    if not primary_command:
   168→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   169→
   170→    estimated_impact = max(
   171→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   172→    )
   173→
   174→    return {
   175→        "id": cluster_name,
   176→        "kind": "cluster",
   177→        "action_type": action_type,
   178→        "summary": summary,
   179→        "members": members,
   180→        "member_count": len(members),
   181→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   182→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   183→        "cluster_auto": True,
   184→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   185→        "confidence": "high",
   186→        "detector": detector,
   187→        "file": "",
   188→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   189→    }
   190→
   191→
   192→def collapse_clusters(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   193→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   194→
   195→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   196→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   197→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   198→    """
   199→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   200→    if not clusters:
   201→        return items
   202→
   203→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   204→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   205→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   206→            continue
   207→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   208→            fid_to_cluster[issue_id] = name
   209→
   210→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   211→        return items
   212→
   213→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   214→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]] = {}
   215→    for item in items:
   216→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   217→        if cname:
   218→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   219→
   220→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   221→    meta_items: dict[str, WorkQueueItem] = {}
   222→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   223→        if len(members) < 2:
   224→            continue
   225→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   226→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   227→        )
   228→
   229→    # Walk in order: replace first member of each collapsed cluster
   230→    # with its meta-item, skip subsequent members
   231→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   232→    result: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   233→    for item in items:
   234→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   235→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   236→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   237→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   238→                result.append(meta_items[cname])
   239→            # skip individual member
   240→        else:
   241→            result.append(item)
   242→    return result
   243→
   244→

   246→    "collapse_clusters",
   247→    "enrich_plan_metadata",
   248→    "filter_cluster_focus",
   249→    "new_item_ids",
   250→    "separate_skipped",
   251→    "stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   252→    "stamp_positions",
   253→]
   254→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13→
    14→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan import stale_policy as stale_policy_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import promoted_insertion_index
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    19→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→

    25→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    26→
    27→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    28→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    29→    "triage::observe",
    30→    "triage::reflect",
    31→    "triage::organize",
    32→    "triage::commit",
    33→)
    34→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    35→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    40→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    41→
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Result dataclasses
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    49→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    50→
    51→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    52→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    53→
    54→    @property
    55→    def changes(self) -> int:
    56→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    57→

    60→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:

    64→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→
    66→    @property
    67→    def changes(self) -> int:
    68→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)

    70→
    71→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→# ID helpers
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    77→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    78→        state,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    84→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    85→
    86→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    87→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    88→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    89→    """
    90→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    91→        state,
    92→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→def current_under_target_ids(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   100→) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   102→
   103→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   104→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   105→    """
   106→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   107→        state,
   108→        target_strict=target_strict,
   109→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   110→    )
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Helpers for sync_stale_dimensions / sync_unscored_dimensions
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→def _has_objective_backlog(
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   120→) -> bool:
   121→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   122→    if policy is not None:
   123→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   124→    return any(
   125→        f.get("status") == "open"
   126→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   127→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   128→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   133→    order: list[str],
   134→    *,
   135→    keep_ids: set[str],
   136→    pruned: list[str],
   137→) -> None:
   138→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   139→    to_remove = [
   140→        fid for fid in order
   141→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   142→        and fid not in keep_ids
   143→    ]
   144→    for fid in to_remove:
   145→        order.remove(fid)
   146→        pruned.append(fid)
   147→
   148→
   149→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   150→    order: list[str],
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    inject_ids: set[str],
   154→    to_front: bool,
   155→    injected: list[str],
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   158→
   159→    When *to_front* is True, inserts after promoted items (front of queue).
   160→    Otherwise appends to the back.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    if to_front:
   164→        insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   165→        for sid in reversed(sorted(inject_ids)):
   166→            if sid not in existing:
   167→                order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   168→                injected.append(sid)
   169→    else:
   170→        for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   171→            if sid not in existing:
   172→                order.append(sid)
   173→                injected.append(sid)
   174→
   175→
   176→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   178→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   179→
   180→def _open_review_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   181→    """Return set of open issue IDs whose detector is 'review' or 'concerns'."""
   182→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   183→    return {
   184→        fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   185→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   186→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   187→    }
   188→
   189→
   190→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   191→    state: StateModel,
   192→    meta: dict,
   193→) -> set[str]:
   194→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   195→    current_review_ids = _open_review_issue_ids(state)
   196→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   197→    return current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   198→
   199→
   200→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   201→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   202→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   203→        while sid in order:
   204→            order.remove(sid)
   205→
   206→
   207→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   208→    order: list[str],
   209→    plan: PlanModel,
   210→    confirmed: set[str],
   211→) -> bool:
   212→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   213→
   214→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   215→    """
   216→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   217→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   218→    existing = set(order)
   219→    injected_count = 0
   220→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   221→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   222→            order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   223→            injected_count += 1
   224→    return injected_count > 0
   225→
   226→
   227→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   228→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   229→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   230→
   231→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   232→    plan: PlanModel,
   233→    state: StateModel,
   234→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   235→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   236→
   237→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   238→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   239→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   240→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   241→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   242→    """
   243→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   244→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   245→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   246→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   247→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   248→
   249→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   250→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   251→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   252→
   253→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   254→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=unscored_ids, to_front=True, injected=result.injected)
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→
   259→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   260→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   261→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   262→
   263→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   264→    plan: PlanModel,
   265→    state: StateModel,
   266→    *,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   270→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   271→
   272→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   273→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   274→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   275→    2. Inject stale and under-target dimension IDs when either:
   276→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle: append to back), OR
   277→       b. A cycle just completed (post-cycle: insert at front so subjective
   278→          review takes priority over new objective issues).
   279→    """
   280→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   281→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   282→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   283→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   284→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   285→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   286→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   287→
   288→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   289→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   290→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   291→
   292→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   293→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   294→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   295→
   296→    if not should_inject:
   297→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   298→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   299→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   300→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   301→        to_evict = [
   302→            fid for fid in order
   303→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   304→            and fid in injectable_ids
   305→        ]
   306→        for fid in to_evict:
   307→            order.remove(fid)
   308→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   309→
   310→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   311→        to_front = cycle_just_completed and has_real_items
   312→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=injectable_ids, to_front=to_front, injected=result.injected)
   313→
   314→    return result
   315→
   316→
   317→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   318→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   319→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   320→
   321→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   322→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   323→
   324→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   325→    """
   326→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   327→
   328→
   329→@dataclass
   330→class TriageSyncResult:
   331→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   332→
   333→    injected: bool = False
   334→    pruned: bool = False
   335→
   336→    @property
   337→    def changes(self) -> int:
   338→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   339→
   340→
   341→def sync_triage_needed(
   342→    plan: PlanModel,
   343→    state: StateModel,
   344→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   345→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   346→
   347→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   348→
   349→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   350→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   351→
   352→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   353→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   354→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   355→    """
   356→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   357→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   358→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   359→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   360→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   361→
   362→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   363→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   364→
   365→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   366→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   367→
   368→    if already_present:
   369→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   370→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   371→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   372→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   373→        # user-started triage session.
   374→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   375→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   376→
   377→            if not new_since_triage:
   378→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   379→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   380→                if current_hash:
   381→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   382→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   383→                result.pruned = True
   384→        return result
   385→
   386→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   387→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   388→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   389→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   390→
   391→        if new_since_triage:
   392→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   393→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, plan, confirmed)
   394→        else:
   395→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   396→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   397→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   398→
   399→    return result
   400→
   401→
   402→@dataclass
   403→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   404→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   405→
   406→    injected: bool = False
   407→
   408→    @property
   409→    def changes(self) -> int:
   410→        return int(self.injected)
   411→
   412→
   413→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   414→    plan: PlanModel,
   415→    state: StateModel,
   416→    *,
   417→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   418→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   419→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   420→
   421→    Injects when:
   422→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   423→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   424→
   425→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   426→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   427→    """
   428→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   429→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   430→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   431→
   432→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   433→        return result
   434→
   435→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   436→    if policy is not None:
   437→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   438→            return result
   439→    else:
   440→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   441→        if unscored:
   442→            return result
   443→
   444→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   445→    insert_at = 0
   446→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   447→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   448→            insert_at = i + 1
   449→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   450→    result.injected = True
   451→    return result
   452→
   453→
   454→@dataclass
   455→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   456→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   457→
   458→    injected: bool = False
   459→
   460→    @property
   461→    def changes(self) -> int:
   462→        return int(self.injected)
   463→
   464→
   465→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   466→    plan: PlanModel,
   467→    state: StateModel,
   468→    *,
   469→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   470→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   471→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   472→
   473→    Only injects when:
   474→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   475→    - At least one objective issue exists
   476→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   477→    - No triage stages are pending
   478→    """
   479→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   480→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   481→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   482→
   483→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   484→        return result
   485→
   486→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   487→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   488→        return result
   489→
   490→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   491→    if policy is not None:
   492→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   493→            return result
   494→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   495→    else:
   496→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   497→        if unscored:
   498→            return result
   499→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   500→        has_objective = any(
   501→            f.get("status") == "open"
   502→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   503→            for f in issues.values()
   504→        )
   505→    if not has_objective:
   506→        return result
   507→
   508→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   509→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   510→    insert_at = 0
   511→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   512→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   513→            insert_at = i + 1
   514→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   515→    result.injected = True
   516→    return result
   517→
   518→
   519→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   520→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   521→
   522→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   523→    """
   524→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   525→
   526→
   527→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   528→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   529→
   530→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   531→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   532→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   533→    resolved since then.
   534→
   535→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   536→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   537→    through the plan.
   538→    """
   539→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   540→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state, triage_ids=TRIAGE_IDS)
   541→
   542→
   543→@dataclass
   544→class ImportScoresSyncResult:
   545→    """What changed during an import-scores sync."""
   546→
   547→    injected: bool = False
   548→
   549→    @property
   550→    def changes(self) -> int:
   551→        return int(self.injected)
   552→
   553→
   554→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   555→    plan: PlanModel,
   556→    state: StateModel,
   557→    *,
   558→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   559→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   560→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   561→
   562→    Only injects when:
   563→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   564→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   565→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   566→
   567→    Positioned after score-checkpoint, before create-plan.
   568→    """
   569→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   570→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   571→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   572→
   573→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   574→        return result
   575→
   576→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   577→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   578→        return result
   579→
   580→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items
   581→    insert_at = 0
   582→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   583→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   584→            insert_at = i + 1
   585→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   586→    result.injected = True
   587→    return result
   588→
   589→
   590→@dataclass
   591→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   592→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   593→
   594→    injected: bool = False
   595→
   596→    @property
   597→    def changes(self) -> int:
   598→        return int(self.injected)
   599→
   600→
   601→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   602→    plan: PlanModel,
   603→    state: StateModel,
   604→    *,
   605→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   606→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   607→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   608→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   609→
   610→    Injects when either:
   611→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   612→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   613→
   614→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   615→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan.
   616→    """
   617→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   618→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   619→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   620→
   621→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   622→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   623→        return result
   624→
   625→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   626→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   627→
   628→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   629→    if not should_inject:
   630→        if policy is not None:
   631→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   632→        else:
   633→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   634→
   635→    if not should_inject:
   636→        return result
   637→
   638→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   639→    insert_at = 0
   640→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   641→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   642→            insert_at = i + 1
   643→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   644→    result.injected = True
   645→    return result
   646→
   647→
   648→__all__ = [
   649→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   650→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   651→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   652→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   653→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   654→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   655→    "WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID",
   656→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   657→    "WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID",
   658→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   659→    "WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID",
   660→    "CommunicateScoreSyncResult",
   661→    "CreatePlanSyncResult",
   662→    "ImportScoresSyncResult",
   663→    "ScoreCheckpointSyncResult",
   664→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   665→    "TriageSyncResult",
   666→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   667→    "current_under_target_ids",
   668→    "current_unscored_ids",

## Analysis: Plan-Aware Sort Keys and Triage/Workflow Interaction

Here's the actual code from all four locations:

---

### 1. **`_natural_sort_key()` function** (lines 189-238 in ranking.py)

```python
def _natural_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple:
    """Compute natural (non-plan-aware) ranking for queue items."""
    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")

    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority
    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
        return (_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))

    # Triage stage items: stage order, blocked after unblocked
    if kind == "workflow_stage":
        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
        stage_index = _workflow_stage_index(item)
        return (_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))

    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan)
    if kind == "workflow_action":
        return (_RANK_WORKFLOW, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))

    if kind == "cluster":
        # Clusters sort before individual issues, ordered by action type
        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
        )
        return (
            _RANK_CLUSTER,
            action_pri,
            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
            item.get("id", ""),
        )

    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)

    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
        return (
            _RANK_ISSUE,
            -impact,
            subjective_score_value(item),
            item.get("id", ""),
        )

    detail = detail_dict(item)
    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    return (
        _RANK_ISSUE,
        -impact,
        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
        -review_weight,
        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
        item.get("id", ""),
    )
```

**Ranking constants:**
```python
_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3  # Unassessed subjective dimensions
_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -2    # Epic triage workflow stages
_RANK_WORKFLOW = -1         # Score checkpoints, create-plan
_RANK_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered issues
_RANK_ISSUE = 1             # Individual issues + assessed subjective
```

**Key insight:** Triage stages (`workflow_stage` kind) have their own natural rank (-2) and sort by blocked-before-unblocked, then by stage index (observe → reflect → organize → commit).

---

### 2. **`item_sort_key()` function** (lines 241-264 in ranking.py)

```python
def item_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple:
    """Unified sort key: plan position first, then natural ranking.

    When ``_plan_position`` is stamped (by :func:`stamp_plan_sort_keys`),
    planned items sort first in plan order, then existing items by natural
    ranking, then newly-discovered items by natural ranking.

    When no plan fields are stamped, falls back to pure natural ranking.
    """
    plan_pos = item.get("_plan_position")

    if plan_pos is not None:
        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
        # Triage stages: even when explicitly planned, maintain the
        # blocked-before-unblocked invariant for correctness.
        if kind == "workflow_stage":
            blocked = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
            stage_idx = _workflow_stage_index(item)
            return (_TIER_PLANNED, blocked, stage_idx, item.get("id", ""))
        return (_TIER_PLANNED, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))

    is_new = item.get("_is_new", False)
    group = _TIER_NEW if is_new else _TIER_EXISTING
    return (group, *_natural_sort_key(item))
```

**Plan tier constants:**
```python
_TIER_PLANNED = 0   # Items with explicit plan position
_TIER_EXISTING = 1  # Known items, natural ranking
_TIER_NEW = 2       # Newly discovered items
```

**Critical observation:** Even when planned (`_plan_position` is not None), **triage stages get special treatment**. They ignore the plan position and sort by `(_TIER_PLANNED, blocked, stage_idx, id)` instead of `(_TIER_PLANNED, 0, plan_pos, id)`. This maintains the blocked-before-unblocked invariant regardless of plan position.

---

### 3. **`stamp_plan_sort_keys()` function** (lines 56-78 in plan_order.py)

```python
def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    plan: dict,
    new_ids: set[str],
) -> None:
    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.

    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    """
    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())

    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(queue_order):
        if issue_id not in skipped_ids:
            position_map[issue_id] = idx

    for item in items:
        item_id = item["id"]
        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
```

**Key behavior:** 
- Walks `queue_order` (which contains ALL item IDs: triage stages, workflow actions, issues)
- Builds a position map, **skipping items that are in the `skipped` dict**
- Both triage stage IDs (e.g., `"triage::observe"`) and workflow action IDs (e.g., `"workflow::create-plan"`) are treated identically here
- The function doesn't differentiate; it just stamps `_plan_position` with their index in `queue_order`
- Items not in `queue_order` get `_plan_position = None`

---

### 4. **`promoted_insertion_index()` function** (lines 37-46 in promoted_ids.py)

```python
def promoted_insertion_index(order: list[str], plan: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
    """Return insertion index immediately after the last promoted item."""
    promoted = set(_promoted_ids(plan))
    if not promoted:
        return 0
    last_idx = -1
    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(order):
        if issue_id in promoted:
            last_idx = idx
    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
```

**Behavior:**
- Scans through `order` (the `queue_order` list) to find the **last item** that appears in `promoted_ids`
- Returns insertion index **immediately after** that last promoted item
- If no promoted items exist, returns 0 (insert at front)
- If promoted items exist but aren't in the current `order`, returns 0

**Where it's used in `stale_dimensions.py`:**
- Line 164: `_inject_subjective_ids()` calls it to find where to insert unscored dimensions (at the front, after promoted)
- Line 216: `_inject_pending_triage_stages()` calls it to find where to insert triage stages (at the front, after promoted)

---

## Summary: How They Interact

| Component | Behavior |
|-----------|----------|
| **`promoted_insertion_index()`** | Finds the insertion point at the front of `queue_order`, after any user-promoted items |
| **Triage stage injection** | Uses `promoted_insertion_index()` to insert `triage::observe`, `reflect`, `organize`, `commit` at the front |
| **Workflow action injection** | Uses similar front-of-queue insertion for `workflow::create-plan`, `workflow::score-checkpoint`, etc. |
| **`stamp_plan_sort_keys()`** | Treats triage stages and workflow actions **identically** — both get `_plan_position` based on their index in `queue_order` |
| **`item_sort_key()`** | **Exception for triage stages**: Even with a plan position, they sort by blocked-before-unblocked + stage order, NOT by plan position |
| **Workflow actions (non-stages)** | No exception — they respect plan position like regular issues |

**The critical asymmetry:** Triage stages (`kind == "workflow_stage"`) have a correctness constraint that overrides plan position, while workflow actions (`kind == "workflow_action"`) fully respect the plan order.
